\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{I915\_CACHING\_NONE}{I915\_CACHING\_NONE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define I915\+\_\+\+C\+A\+C\+H\+I\+N\+G\+\_\+\+N\+O\+NE~0}

@caching\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} G\+TT caching level to apply or possible return value.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} supported @caching values\+:

I915\+\_\+\+C\+A\+C\+H\+I\+N\+G\+\_\+\+N\+O\+NE\+:

G\+PU access is not coherent with C\+PU caches. Default for machines without an L\+LC. This means manual flushing might be needed, if we want G\+PU access to be coherent.

I915\+\_\+\+C\+A\+C\+H\+I\+N\+G\+\_\+\+C\+A\+C\+H\+ED\+:

G\+PU access is coherent with C\+PU caches and furthermore the data is cached in last-\/level caches shared between C\+PU cores and the G\+PU GT.

I915\+\_\+\+C\+A\+C\+H\+I\+N\+G\+\_\+\+D\+I\+S\+P\+L\+AY\+:

Special G\+PU caching mode which is coherent with the scanout engines. Transparently falls back to I915\+\_\+\+C\+A\+C\+H\+I\+N\+G\+\_\+\+N\+O\+NE on platforms where no special cache mode (like write-\/through or gfdt flushing) is available. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} kernel automatically sets this mode when using a buffer as a scanout target. Userspace can manually set this mode to avoid a costly stall and clflush in the hotpath of drawing the first frame. \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_abd0597f5129e90c3c5d3252a85b8eb05}\label{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_abd0597f5129e90c3c5d3252a85b8eb05}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define I915\+\_\+\+E\+X\+E\+C\+\_\+\+I\+S\+\_\+\+P\+I\+N\+N\+ED~(1$<$$<$10)}

Inform the kernel that the batch is and will always be pinned. This negates the requirement for a workaround to be performed to avoid an incoherent CS (such as can be found on 830/845). If this flag is not passed, the kernel will endeavour to make sure the batch is coherent with the CS before execution. If this flag is passed, userspace assumes the responsibility for ensuring the same. \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_ae7794387d2a98210e98e238384ea07c0}\label{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_ae7794387d2a98210e98e238384ea07c0}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define I915\+\_\+\+G\+E\+M\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+E\+X\+T\+\_\+\+M\+E\+M\+O\+R\+Y\+\_\+\+R\+E\+G\+I\+O\+NS~0}

@extensions\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} chain of extensions to apply to this object.

This will be useful in the future when we need to support several different extensions, and we need to apply more than one when creating the object. See struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structi915__user__extension}{i915\+\_\+user\+\_\+extension}}.

If we don\textquotesingle{}t supply any extensions then we get the same old gem\+\_\+create behaviour.

For I915\+\_\+\+G\+E\+M\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+E\+X\+T\+\_\+\+M\+E\+M\+O\+R\+Y\+\_\+\+R\+E\+G\+I\+O\+NS usage see struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structdrm__i915__gem__create__ext__memory__regions}{drm\+\_\+i915\+\_\+gem\+\_\+create\+\_\+ext\+\_\+memory\+\_\+regions}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_a4e24e09ae78f81bcb2131dc83174f251}\label{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_a4e24e09ae78f81bcb2131dc83174f251}}

\index{I915\_L3\_PARITY\_UEVENT@{I915\_L3\_PARITY\_UEVENT}!i915\_drm.h@{i915\_drm.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{I915\_L3\_PARITY\_UEVENT}{I915\_L3\_PARITY\_UEVENT}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define I915\+\_\+\+L3\+\_\+\+P\+A\+R\+I\+T\+Y\+\_\+\+U\+E\+V\+E\+NT~\char`\"{}L3\+\_\+\+P\+A\+R\+I\+T\+Y\+\_\+\+E\+R\+R\+OR\char`\"{}}

D\+OC\+: uevents generated by i915 on it\textquotesingle{}s device node

I915\+\_\+\+L3\+\_\+\+P\+A\+R\+I\+T\+Y\+\_\+\+U\+E\+V\+E\+NT -\/ Generated when the driver receives a parity mismatch event from the gpu l3 cache. Additional information supplied is R\+OW, B\+A\+NK, S\+U\+B\+B\+A\+NK, S\+L\+I\+CE of the affected cacheline. Userspace should keep track of these events and if a specific cache-\/line seems to have a persistent error remap it with the l3 remapping tool supplied in intel-\/gpu-\/tools. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} value supplied with the event is always 1.

I915\+\_\+\+E\+R\+R\+O\+R\+\_\+\+U\+E\+V\+E\+NT -\/ Generated upon error detection, currently only via hangcheck. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} error detection event is a good indicator of when things began to go badly. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} value supplied with the event is a 1 upon error detection, and a 0 upon reset completion, signifying no more error exists. N\+O\+TE\+: Disabling hangcheck or reset via module parameter will cause the related events to not be seen.

I915\+\_\+\+R\+E\+S\+E\+T\+\_\+\+U\+E\+V\+E\+NT -\/ Event is generated just before an attempt to reset the G\+PU. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} value supplied with the event is always 1. N\+O\+TE\+: Disable reset via module parameter will cause this event to not be seen. \mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_a8fc3d3bfd9ee82ef0dae6c8c8945146c}\label{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_a8fc3d3bfd9ee82ef0dae6c8c8945146c}}

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I915\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+P\+\_\+\+O\+A\+\_\+\+E\+X\+P\+O\+N\+E\+NT&Specifying this property implicitly requests periodic OA unit sampling and (at least on Haswell) the sampling frequency is derived from this exponent as follows\+:

80ns $\ast$ 2$^\wedge$(period\+\_\+exponent + 1)

This property is available in perf revision 1. \\
\hline

\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_PROP\_HOLD\_PREEMPTION@{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_PROP\_HOLD\_PREEMPTION}!i915\_drm.h@{i915\_drm.h}}\index{i915\_drm.h@{i915\_drm.h}!DRM\_I915\_PERF\_PROP\_HOLD\_PREEMPTION@{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_PROP\_HOLD\_PREEMPTION}}}\mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_a6cd3b5cba7efaa7722a4b7b7cd97179eaf7f0c052a170246669f4c063c4a60c8b}\label{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_a6cd3b5cba7efaa7722a4b7b7cd97179eaf7f0c052a170246669f4c063c4a60c8b}} 
D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I915\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+P\+\_\+\+H\+O\+L\+D\+\_\+\+P\+R\+E\+E\+M\+P\+T\+I\+ON&Specifying this property is only valid when specify a context to filter with D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I915\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+P\+\_\+\+C\+T\+X\+\_\+\+H\+A\+N\+D\+LE. Specifying this property will hold preemption of the particular context we want to gather performance data about. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} execbuf2 submissions must include a drm\+\_\+i915\+\_\+gem\+\_\+execbuffer\+\_\+ext\+\_\+perf parameter for this to apply.

This property is available in perf revision 3. \\

D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I915\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+P\+\_\+\+G\+L\+O\+B\+A\+L\+\_\+\+S\+S\+EU&Specifying this pins all contexts to the specified S\+S\+EU power configuration for the duration of the recording.

This parameter\textquotesingle{}s value is a pointer to a struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structdrm__i915__gem__context__param__sseu}{drm\+\_\+i915\+\_\+gem\+\_\+context\+\_\+param\+\_\+sseu}}.

This property is available in perf revision 4. \\
\hline

\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_PROP\_POLL\_OA\_PERIOD@{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_PROP\_POLL\_OA\_PERIOD}!i915\_drm.h@{i915\_drm.h}}\index{i915\_drm.h@{i915\_drm.h}!DRM\_I915\_PERF\_PROP\_POLL\_OA\_PERIOD@{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_PROP\_POLL\_OA\_PERIOD}}}\mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_a6cd3b5cba7efaa7722a4b7b7cd97179ea5748f4541cb77885ca5a099f83bc99bf}\label{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_a6cd3b5cba7efaa7722a4b7b7cd97179ea5748f4541cb77885ca5a099f83bc99bf}} 
D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I915\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+P\+\_\+\+P\+O\+L\+L\+\_\+\+O\+A\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+I\+OD&This optional parameter specifies the timer interval in nanoseconds at which the i915 driver will check the OA buffer for available data. Minimum allowed value is 100 microseconds. A default value is used by the driver if this parameter is not specified. Note that larger timer values will reduce cpu consumption during OA perf captures. However, excessively large values would potentially result in OA buffer overwrites as captures reach end of the OA buffer.

This property is available in perf revision 5. \\
\hline

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{drm\_i915\_perf\_record\_type}{drm\_i915\_perf\_record\_type}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_aaf28c5e4db7592a09bfdf077dcdafa3f}{drm\+\_\+i915\+\_\+perf\+\_\+record\+\_\+type}}}

\begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}
\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_RECORD\_SAMPLE@{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_RECORD\_SAMPLE}!i915\_drm.h@{i915\_drm.h}}\index{i915\_drm.h@{i915\_drm.h}!DRM\_I915\_PERF\_RECORD\_SAMPLE@{DRM\_I915\_PERF\_RECORD\_SAMPLE}}}\mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_aaf28c5e4db7592a09bfdf077dcdafa3fa8b37a8fae3e576b2f5a5573ad3d6e74e}\label{uapi_2drm_2i915__drm_8h_aaf28c5e4db7592a09bfdf077dcdafa3fa8b37a8fae3e576b2f5a5573ad3d6e74e}} 
D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I915\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+F\+\_\+\+R\+E\+C\+O\+R\+D\+\_\+\+S\+A\+M\+P\+LE&Samples are the work horse record type whose contents are extensible and defined when opening an i915 perf stream based on the given properties.

Boolean properties following the naming convention D\+R\+M\+\_\+\+I915\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+F\+\_\+\+S\+A\+M\+P\+L\+E\+\_\+xyz\+\_\+\+P\+R\+OP request the inclusion of \textquotesingle{}xyz\textquotesingle{} data in every sample.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} order of these sample properties given by userspace has no affect on the ordering of data within a sample. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} order is documented here.

struct \{ struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structdrm__i915__perf__record__header}{drm\+\_\+i915\+\_\+perf\+\_\+record\+\_\+header}} header;
